It includes immutable and extended data structures for efficient lookup.\\n\\n4. **Role Graph Resolution**: The role graph is implemented as `map[string]roleGraphEdgesTable` in MySQLPrivilege. Three key methods handle role resolution:\\n   - `FindAllRole()`: Breadth-first search through role graph to find all transitive roles\\n   - `FindAllUserEffectiveRoles()`: Filters and returns only granted roles\\n   - `FindRole()`: Detects direct edges between users and roles\\n\\n5. **Request Verification Flow**: Both UserPrivileges.RequestVerification and MySQLPrivilege.RequestVerification check privileges hierarchically:\\n   - User-level global privileges\\n   - Database-level privileges\\n   - Table-level privileges\\n   - Column-level privileges\\n\\n**SQL Statement Privilege Mapping:**\\n\\nThe planner integrates privilege checking at multiple stages:\\n\\n1. **PlanBuilder** (pkg/planner/core/planbuilder.go): Defines `visitInfo` struct containing:\\n   - `privilege`: Required privilege type (e.g., mysql.SelectPriv, mysql.InsertPriv, mysql.UpdatePriv, mysql.DeletePriv)\\n   - `db`, `table`, `column`: Resource scope\\n   - `dynamicPrivs`: Optional dynamic privilege names\\n   \\nPrivileges are added per statement type (SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE, CREATE, etc.) via `appendVisitInfo()` and `appendDynamicVisitInfo()`.\\n\\n2. **Logical Plan Builder** (pkg/planner/core/logical_plan_builder.go): For views and tables, adds specific privilege checks:\\n   - SELECT operations: Adds mysql.SelectPriv\\n   - INSERT operations: Adds mysql.InsertPriv\\n   - UPDATE operations: Adds mysql.UpdatePriv\\n   - DELETE operations: Adds mysql.DeletePriv\\n   - For views with SECURITY DEFINER: Uses RequestVerificationWithUser() to check definer's privileges\\n\\n3. **Optimizer** (pkg/planner/core/optimizer.go): `CheckPrivilege()` function verifies all accumulated visitInfo entries via RequestVerification(). Connection-time verification via `ConnectionVerification()`\\n2. Statement planning collects required privileges in visitInfo\\n3. Planner preprocessing validates table access\\n4. Optimizer calls `CheckPrivilege()` to verify all visitInfo entries\\n5. For each visitInfo: RequestVerification checks user's role hierarchy and accumulates privileges from global → database → table → column scopes\\n6. Dynamic privileges (BACKUP_ADMIN, RESTORE_ADMIN, etc.) are checked separately via RequestDynamicVerification()\\n\\n**RBAC Implementation Details:**\\n\\nThe role-based access control uses breadth-first search through the role graph stored in mysql.role_edges table. FindAllRole() starting from a user's active roles discovers all transitive role memberships. Privileges are accumulated from all roles in the effective role set, enabling hierarchical privilege inheritance.\"\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":6,"cache_creation_input_tokens":605,"cache_read_input_tokens":69303,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":605,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1d341600-5452-46ef-bca5-e8f29ce59de8","uuid":"12b67f36-610f-4d96-8373-8281758f8825"}
